594 comparisons among the 293 accepted declarations. [Source hashes and review provenance](Separation--Taylor.json).

| `MvPolynomial.taylor` | `Roth.coeffNone` | no | A: Defines multivariate polynomial translation by a vector as a bundled coefficient-algebra endomorphism. Mechanism: The universal evaluation homomorphism aeval substitutes X j+C(α j) for each variable, automatically preserving sums, products, and coefficients. B: Extract the polynomial coefficient of a fixed power of the distinguished variable. Mechanism: Compose the standard optionEquivLeft algebra equivalence with Polynomial.coeff. Translation expands around an arbitrary point, preserving the same multivariate polynomial space. Distinguished-variable extraction views a polynomial as univariate with polynomial coefficients, then forms a basis of their span and separates variables. These are different coordinate decompositions; Taylor coefficient/Hasse evaluation is not the coefficient-span basis or primitive factorization theorem. | Use existing coefficient extraction/substitution homomorphism laws in both settings, keeping translation and separated tensor-style decomposition APIs distinct. |
